# Run 2 working notes

Started Monday 9 November 2026, about 09:13 CET. Hard deadline Wednesday 11 November 2026 07:00 Europe/Zurich. Goal remains active. Work is confined to gpt2/, offline, CPU six threads, one heavy process at a time. Parent model.py, REPORT.md, NOTES.md and .venv were read/used under GOAL.md's explicit exception. No subagents or apps.

## Frozen protocol — do not change

- Unmodified model.py has required SHA-256 7c01703240dbec5d554527dc666e35b3df8391d0b117fddc07afcf325a21d11c.
- `experiments/prepare_host.py` imported the local safetensors with numpy, transposed all four Conv1D weight types, skipped 12 attention masks, tied lm_head to wte, and strict-loaded all 149 state keys. `host/ckpt.pt` was saved once. No training.
- Pure stdlib Unicode pretokenizer plus byte BPE, local vocab/merges. Known IDs, Unicode/whitespace round trips and one-token spaced digits tested.
- `tools/score.py --prepare` alone read heldout text. Gutenberg headers/footers stripped; WikiText detokenized and headings/unknown paragraphs dropped. Cached tokens and raw/clean hashes recorded in host/text_manifest.json. Do not open host/heldout_tokens.npz or heldout files outside score.py.
- Sources, host, tokenizer assets and calibration data sealed in tools/frozen.json. Never modify tools/, model.py, host, test data, or ledger directly now. The meters append hash-chained events and enforce quotas and sparse edit rules.
- Eligible internal parameters 85,054,464; at most 4,252,723 changed scalars. wte, lm_head, wpe, ln_f fully protected. Max absolute value 1e6; finite float32 only; exact whole-tensor rescalings refused.
- Inference only via forward.py or score.py. Weight/activation arithmetic in compiler scripts is allowed. Builder scripts and all helper sources must be immutable after first metered checkpoint; each new version gets a new filename.
- Spaced exact-match scoring checks all correct-prefix argmaxes in parallel, logically identical to greedy sequence equality. Natural scoring generates greedily, up to eight tokens, comparing decoded text. Synthetic oracle tests covered wrong intermediate digits, wrong newline and alternate BPE segmentation before freeze.
- A forward generation step costs one of 300 forward units. Multi-rung scoring costs one quick/full run per rung, with a shared text evaluation. Quick: first 2,000 problems and 256 text windows. Full: every problem, 2,048 text windows, three noise levels and repeated-problem diagnostics. The actual fresh test seeds are private to score.py.

## Baselines and first capture jobs

`artifacts/baseline_quick.json` completed: G1 0/100; G2 0/2,000; G3 0/2,000; G4 0/2,000; G5 6/2,000. Text self agreement 100%, KL 0, CE difference 0, host CE 4.2740137822 nats over 16,384 next-token targets. No rung passes.

Job started as exec session 4588, sequentially:
1. host English sanity, three equal-length prompts, twelve greedy tokens (12 forward units), requests/host_english.json.
2. calibration 64x64 tokens, captures all 12 layers' ln1, ln2, raw pre-ln2 residual, GELU and attention projection input; artifacts/host_calib64.pt (~1.2 GB), one forward unit.
3. G1 **format only** examples: all first/second digits, two leading-symbol choices, independent synthetic continuation digit; no correct-answer labels. 200 sequences of six tokens, same captures, artifacts/host_format_g1.pt, one forward unit.

Current next step: inspect these recorded activations, choose weak MLP capacity and assess a whole-equation format detector. No edited candidates yet. Candidate design must derive arithmetic from per-digit/per-place logic, never fit whole-problem answers. Track every scale trial (maximum five per scale per version). Do not stop merely because a rung is hard; try three genuinely different designs before declaring infeasibility.

## Required finalization

Update REPORT.md at each passed rung and at least every three hours. Bank passing full-score checkpoints in best/Gn with delta, score, manifest and description. Reserve full runs for one-shot `tools/verify.py`, which invokes score.py's fresh seed and checks bank hashes. No circuit changes after final verification. Export run2.zip (<300 MB, no activation artifacts or host weights) plus safetensors pieces of at most 300 MB. Keep gpt2 under 25 GB and delete only own superseded artifacts if needed.

## 10:35 CET — first complete G1 circuit, close but not passing

- English sanity completions were fluent. Baseline and all frozen sources remain unchanged.
- Budgets before the currently queued diagnostics: 18 forward units, 7 quick units, 0 full units. Current session 73065 queues one diagnostic forward, an A3 frontend build, and two more forward captures, bringing forwards to 21 if all finish. No banked rung yet.
- `experiments/analyze_geometry_v1.py` measured all 12 layers on the initial 64x64 text capture. `analyze_format_gates_v2.py` built format-only Fisher discriminants at layer 10 using 512x64 calibration tokens and 512 malformed-equation negatives. Three stage gates have positive gaps 0.2759, 0.1352, 0.05686. Controls corrupt each operand/operator role; detector targets are format and stage, never answers.
- **Design A frontend** (`compile_a_frontend_v1.py`, immutable after metering): six weak MLP10 units implement saturated stage gates; five weak residual directions lie in the nullspace of recorded equals/first-output states and removed output columns. Two attention heads (6 and 9) independently read the current digit at operand positions. The decoder uses one-hot current-token labels and position flags, not sums. Q/K and V/output rotations preserve the host before replacing weak channels. The last attention output projection is orthogonalized against the five memory directions. Changed 894,469 scalars, max absolute edited weight 1008.196.
- A1 frontend scale values: carrier=16384, attention score=64, value gain=.25; gate sharpness is analytically 48/separation gap. Its quick G1 score is not an arithmetic pass (no output program), but text passes: agreement .9863891602, KL .0043451543, CE +.0054963001.
- Recorded A1 sums had worst error .1271 at equals and .2377 at first output; constant register should be .25 but ranges .2430–.2553 at first output. Large carrier and cancelling gates amplify float32 errors. Carrier-corrected norms also vary (sigma/sigma0 1.00035–1.01311).
- `analyze_a1_off_and_readout.py` derives a text-positive format-off direction, exactly orthogonal to 300 recorded active arithmetic states. Calibration text range .9232–1.0389. Program stage 2 has positive off values ~.885, so arithmetic logic stays off there. A frozen-unembedding covariance solve gives each digit/newline a positive ideal logit margin (minimum 1.3079 nats). Ridges: off detector 1e-6 of trace; output covariance 1e-5.
- **Design A output** (`compile_a_output_v1.py`, failed builds; v2 separates newline temperature): 44 neurons implement decimal sum thresholds, units modulo ten, carry and newline. All arithmetic is per-column hand logic. Text capacity is freed by a covariance projection reconstructing removed GELU features from retained ones plus their means; no arithmetic-answer solve.
- Scale attempts: main temperature 64 rejected before saving (FC weight 4,627,363.5 exceeds 1e6). Temperature 8 in v1 fails the text-off certificate because newline GELU is insufficiently saturated. V2 fixes newline temperature at 64; main 8 rejected before saving (FC bias 1,108,865.875). Main 4, newline 64, output gain 65536, stage penalty 256, capacity ridge .001 produced `experiments/a2t4_g1/delta.pt`. These are three distinct main-temperature values, within five. The large text-off penalty (~70,770) is computed from activation bounds, not tuned against arithmetic outputs.
- **A2T4 quick G1:** 99/100 exact; sole reported failure 9+7 gives ` 1 3` instead of ` 1 6`. Text agreement .9821166992 (fails .985), KL .0047182679, CE +.0056468488 (both pass). 3,288,368 changed scalars; max |w| 554,458.375. No full run or bank.
- Current work: capture actual complete-circuit activations on 100 correct-prefix G1 cases (diagnostic use only, never fitting answer labels). Build A3 frontend with carrier 4096 (second carrier value), keeping attention score/value gain unchanged. Smaller carrier should reduce finite-precision register contamination; stage gates still dominate host residuals. Capture A3 format/calibration activations next. Improve MLP capacity selection from raw variance to conditional covariance and frozen readout geometry; this is text/weight analysis only.
- All builders and manifests remain versioned. Failed build logs and auxiliaries are retained. Do not modify any source named by a metered checkpoint manifest.
